eu.maveniverse.domtrip.maven.MavenPomElements.ModelVersions.MODEL_VERSION_4_1_0;\n37: \n38: /**\n39:  * Base class for upgrade goals containing shared functionality.\n40:  * Subclasses only differ in whether they save modifications to disk.\n41:  *\n42:  * <h2>Supported Upgrades</h2>\n43:  *\n44:  * <h3>Model Version Upgrades</h3>\n45:  * <ul>\n46:  *   <li><strong>4.0.0 → 4.1.0</strong>: Upgrades Maven 3.x POMs to Maven 4.1.0 format</li>\n47:  * </ul>\n48:  *\n49:  * <h3>4.0.0 → 4.1.0 Upgrade Process</h3>\n50:  * <ol>\n51:  *   <li><strong>Namespace Update</strong>: Changes namespace from Maven 4.0.0 to 4.1.0 for all elements</li>\n52:  *   <li><strong>Schema Location Update</strong>: Updates xsi:schemaLocation to Maven 4.1.0 XSD</li>\n53:  *   <li><strong>Module Conversion</strong>: Converts {@code <modules>} to {@code <subprojects>} and {@code <module>} to {@code <subproject>}</li>\n54:  *   <li><strong>Model Version Update</strong>: Updates {@code <modelVersion>} to 4.1.0</li>\n55:  * </ol>\n56:  *\n57:  * <h3>Default Behavior</h3>\n58:  * If no specific options are provided, the tool applies {@code --fix-model} and {@code --plugins} by default to ensure Maven 4 compatibility.\n59:  *\n60:  * <h3>All-in-One Option</h3>\n61:  * The {@code --all} option is a convenience flag equivalent to {@code --model 4.1.0 --infer --fix-model --plugins}.\n62:  * It performs a complete upgrade to Maven 4.1.0 with all optimizations, compatibility fixes, and plugin upgrades.\n63:  *\n64:  * <h3>Maven 4 Compatibility Fixes</h3>\n65:  * When {@code --fix-model} option is enabled (or by default), applies fixes for Maven 4 compatibility issues:\n66:  * <ul>\n67:  *   <li><strong>Unsupported combine.children Attributes</strong>: Changes 'override' to 'merge' (Maven 4 only supports 'append' and 'merge')</li>\n68:  *   <li><strong>Unsupported combine.self Attributes</strong>: Changes 'append' to 'merge' (Maven 4 only supports 'override', 'merge', and 'remove')</li>\n69:  *   <li><strong>Duplicate Dependencies</strong>: Removes duplicate dependency declarations that Maven 4 strictly validates</li>\n70:  *   <li><strong>Duplicate Plugins</strong>: Removes duplicate plugin declarations that Maven 4 strictly validates</li>\n71:  *   <li><strong>Unsupported Repository Expressions</strong>: Comments out repositories with expressions not supported by Maven 4</li>\n72:  *   <li><strong>Incorrect Parent Relative Paths</strong>: Fixes parent.relativePath that point to non-existent POMs by searching the project structure</li>\n73:  *   <li><strong>.mvn Directory Creation</strong>: Creates .mvn directory in root when not upgrading to 4.1.0 to avoid root directory warnings</li>\n74:  * </ul>\n75:  *\n76:  * <h3>Plugin Upgrades</h3>\n77:  * When {@code --plugins} option is enabled (or by default), upgrades plugins known to fail with Maven 4:\n78:  * <ul>\n79:  *   <li><strong>maven-exec-plugin</strong>: Upgrades to version 3.2.0 or higher</li>\n80:  *   <li><strong>maven-enforcer-plugin</strong>: Upgrades to version 3.0.0 or higher</li>\n81:  *   <li><strong>flatten-maven-plugin</strong>: Upgrades to version 1.2.7 or higher</li>\n82:  *   <li><strong>maven-shade-plugin</strong>: Upgrades to version 3.5.0 or higher</li>\n83:  *   <li><strong>maven-remote-resources-plugin</strong>: Upgrades to version 3.0.0 or higher</li>\n84:  * </ul>\n85:  * Plugin versions are upgraded in both {@code <build><plugins>} and {@code <build><pluginManagement><plugins>} sections.\n86:  * If a plugin version is defined via a property, the property value is updated instead.\n87:  *\n88:  * <h3>Inference Optimizations (Optional)</h3>\n89:  * When {@code --infer} option is enabled, applies inference optimizations to remove redundant information:\n90:  *\n91:  * <h4>Limited Inference for 4.0.0 Models (Maven 3.x POMs)</h4>\n92:  * <ul>\n93:  *   <li><strong>Child GroupId Removal</strong>: Removes child {@code <groupId>} when it matches parent groupId</li>\n94:  *   <li><strong>Child Version Removal</strong>: Removes child {@code <version>} when it matches parent version</li>\n95:  * </ul>\n96:  *\n97:  * <h4>Full Inference for 4.1.0+ Models</h4>\n98:  * <ul>\n99:  *   <li><strong>ModelVersion Removal</strong>: Removes {@code <modelVersion>} element (inference enabled)</li>\n100:  *   <li><strong>Root Attribute</strong>: Adds {@code root=\"true\"} attribute to root project</li>\n101:  *   <li><strong>Parent Element Trimming</strong>:\n102:  *     <ul>\n103:  *       <li>Removes parent {@code <groupId>} when child has no explicit groupId</li>\n104:  *       <li>Removes parent {@code <version>} when child has no explicit version</li>\n105:  *       <li>Removes parent {@code <artifactId>} See the License for the\n * specific language governing permissions and limitations\n * under the License.\n */\npackage org.apache.maven.cling.invoker.mvnup.goals;\n\nimport eu.maveniverse.domtrip.DomTripException;\nimport eu.maveniverse.domtrip.maven.MavenPomElements;\nimport org.apache.maven.api.cli.mvnup.UpgradeOptions;\nimport org.apache.maven.api.di.Inject;\nimport static eu.maveniverse.domtrip.maven.MavenPomElements.ModelVersions.MODEL_VERSION_4_1_0;\n\n/**\n * Base class for upgrade goals containing shared functionality.\n * Subclasses only differ in whether they save modifications to disk.\n *\n * <h2>Supported Upgrades</h2>\n *\n * <h3>Model Version Upgrades</h3>\n * <ul>\n *   <li><strong>4.0.0 → 4.1.0</strong>: Upgrades Maven 3.x POMs to Maven 4.1.0 format</li>\n * </ul>\n *\n * <h3>4.0.0 → 4.1.0 Upgrade Process</h3>\n * <ol>\n *   <li><strong>Namespace Update</strong>: Changes namespace from Maven 4.0.0 to 4.1.0 for all elements</li>\n *   <li><strong>Schema Location Update</strong>: Updates xsi:schemaLocation to Maven 4.1.0 XSD</li>\n *   <li><strong>Module Conversion</strong>: Converts {@code <modules>} to {@code <subprojects>} and {@code <module>} to {@code <subproject>}</li>\n *   <li><strong>Model Version Update</strong>: Updates {@code <modelVersion>} to 4.1.0</li>\n * </ol>\n *\n * <h3>Default Behavior</h3>\n * If no specific options are provided, the tool applies {@code --fix-model} and {@code --plugins} by default to ensure Maven 4 compatibility.\n *\n * <h3>All-in-One Option</h3>\n * The {@code --all} option is a convenience flag equivalent to {@code --model 4.1.0 --infer --fix-model --plugins}.\n * It performs a complete upgrade to Maven 4.1.0 with all optimizations, compatibility fixes, and plugin upgrades.\n *\n * <h3>Maven 4 Compatibility Fixes</h3>\n * When {@code --fix-model} option is enabled (or by default), applies fixes for Maven 4 compatibility issues:\n * <ul>\n *   <li><strong>Unsupported combine.children Attributes</strong>: Changes 'override' to 'merge' (Maven 4 only supports 'append' and 'merge')</li>\n *   <li><strong>Unsupported combine.self Attributes</strong>: Changes 'append' to 'merge' (Maven 4 only supports 'override', 'merge', and 'remove')</li>\n *   <li><strong>Duplicate Dependencies</strong>: Removes duplicate dependency declarations that Maven 4 strictly validates</li>\n *   <li><strong>Duplicate Plugins</strong>: Removes duplicate plugin declarations that Maven 4 strictly validates</li>\n *   <li><strong>Unsupported Repository Expressions</strong>: Comments out repositories with expressions not supported by Maven 4</li>\n *   <li><strong>Incorrect Parent Relative Paths</strong>: Fixes parent.relativePath that point to non-existent POMs by searching the project structure</li>\n *   <li><strong>.mvn Directory Creation</strong>: Creates .mvn directory in root when not upgrading to 4.1.0 to avoid root directory warnings</li>\n * </ul>\n *\n * <h3>Plugin Upgrades</h3>\n * When {@code --plugins} option is enabled (or by default), upgrades plugins known to fail with Maven 4:\n * <ul>\n *   <li><strong>maven-exec-plugin</strong>: Upgrades to version 3.2.0 or higher</li>\n *   <li><strong>maven-enforcer-plugin</strong>: Upgrades to version 3.0.0 or higher</li>\n *   <li><strong>flatten-maven-plugin</strong>: Upgrades to version 1.2.7 or higher</li>\n *   <li><strong>maven-shade-plugin</strong>: Upgrades to version 3.5.0 or higher</li>\n *   <li><strong>maven-remote-resources-plugin</strong>: Upgrades to version 3.0.0 or higher</li>\n * </ul>\n * Plugin versions are upgraded in both {@code <build><plugins>} and {@code <build><pluginManagement><plugins>} sections.\n * If a plugin version is defined via a property, the property value is updated instead.\n *\n * <h3>Inference Optimizations (Optional)</h3>\n * When {@code --infer} option is enabled, applies inference optimizations to remove redundant information:\n *\n * <h4>Limited Inference for 4.0.0 Models (Maven 3.x POMs)</h4>\n * <ul>\n *   <li><strong>Child GroupId Removal</strong>: Removes child {@code <groupId>} when it matches parent groupId</li>\n *   <li><strong>Child Version Removal</strong>: Removes child {@code <version>} when it matches parent version</li>\n * </ul>\n *\n * <h4>Full Inference for 4.1.0+ Models</h4>\n * <ul>\n *   <li><strong>ModelVersion Removal</strong>: Removes {@code <modelVersion>} element (inference enabled)</li>\n *   <li><strong>Root Attribute</strong>: Adds {@code root=\"true\"} attribute to root project</li>\n *   <li><strong>Parent Element Trimming</strong>:\n *     <ul>\n *       <li>Removes parent {@code <groupId>} when child has no explicit groupId</li>\n *       <li>Removes parent {@code <version>} when child has no explicit version</li>\n *       <li>Removes parent {@code <artifactId>} when it can be inferred from relativePath</li>\n *     </ul>\n *   </li>\n *   <li><strong>Managed Dependencies Cleanup</strong>: Removes managed dependencies pointing to project artifacts</li>\n *   <li><strong>Dependency Inference</strong>:\n *     <ul>\n *       <li>Removes dependency {@code <version>} when it points to a project artifact</li>\n *       <li>Removes dependency {@code <groupId>} when it points to a project artifact</li>\n *       <li>Applies to main dependencies, profile dependencies, and plugin dependencies</li>\n *     </ul>\n *   </li>\n *   <li><strong>Subprojects List Removal</strong>: Removes redundant {@code <subprojects>} lists that match direct child directories</li>\n * </ul>\n *\n * <h3>Multi-Module Project Support</h3>\n * <ul>\n *   <li><strong>POM Discovery</strong>: Recursively discovers all POM files in the project structure</li>\n *   <li><strong>GAV Resolution</strong>: Computes GroupId, ArtifactId, Version for all project artifacts with parent inheritance</li>\n *   <li><strong>Cross-Module Inference</strong>: Uses knowledge of all project artifacts for intelligent inference decisions</li>\n *   <li><strong>RelativePath Resolution</strong>: Resolves parent POMs via relativePath for artifactId inference</li>\n * </ul>\n *\n * <h3>Format Preservation</h3>\n * <ul>\n *   <li><strong>Whitespace Preservation</strong>: Maintains original formatting when removing elements</li>\n *   <li><strong>Comment Preservation</strong>: Preserves XML comments and processing instructions</li>\n *   <li><strong>Line Separator Handling</strong>: Uses system-appropriate line separators</li>\n * </ul>\n */\npublic abstract class AbstractUpgradeGoal implements Goal {\n\n    private final StrategyOrchestrator orchestrator;\n\n    @Inject\n    public AbstractUpgradeGoal(StrategyOrchestrator orchestrator) {\n        this.orchestrator = orchestrator;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Executes the upgrade goal.\n     * Template method that calls doUpgrade and optionally saves modifications.\n     */\n    @Override\n    public int execute(UpgradeContext context) throws Exception {\n        UpgradeOptions options = context.options();\n\n        // Determine target model version\n        // Default to 4.0.0 unless --all is specified or explicit --model-version is provided\n        String targetModel;\n        if (options.modelVersion().isPresent()) {\n            targetModel = options.modelVersion().get();\n        } else if (options.all().orElse(false)) {\n            targetModel = MODEL_VERSION_4_1_0;\n        } else {\n            targetModel = MavenPomElements.ModelVersions.MODEL_VERSION_4_0_0;\n        }\n\n        if (!ModelVersionUtils.isValidModelVersion(targetModel)) {\n            context.failure(\"Invalid target model version: \" + targetModel);\n            context.failure(\"Supported versions: 4.0.0, 4.1.0\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n\n        // Discover POMs\n        context.info(\"Discovering POM files...\");\n        Path startingDirectory = options.directory().map(Paths::get).orElse(context.invokerRequest.cwd());\n\n        Map<Path, Document> pomMap;\n        try {\n            pomMap = PomDiscovery.discoverPoms(startingDirectory);\n        } catch (IOException | DomTripException e) {\n            context.failure(\"Failed to discover POM files: \" + e.getMessage());\n            return 1;\n        }\n\n        if (pomMap.isEmpty()) {\n            context.warning(\"No POM files found in \" + startingDirectory);\n            return 0;\n        }\n\n        context.info(\"Found \" + pomMap.size() + \" POM file(s)\");\n\n        // Perform the upgrade logic\n        int result = doUpgrade(context, targetModel, pomMap);\n\n        // Save modifications if this is an apply goal\n        if (shouldSaveModifications() && result == 0) {\n            saveModifications(context, pomMap);\n        }\n\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Performs the upgrade logic using the strategy pattern.\n     * Delegates to StrategyOrchestrator for coordinated strategy execution.\n     */\n    protected int doUpgrade(UpgradeContext context, String targetModel, Map<Path, Document> pomMap) {\n        // Execute strategies using the orchestrator\n        try {\n            UpgradeResult result = orchestrator.executeStrategies(context, pomMap);\n\n            // Create .mvn directory if needed to avoid root directory warnings\n            // This is needed for both 4.0.0 and 4.1.0 to help Maven find the project root\n            createMvnDirectoryIfNeeded(context);\n\n            return result.errorPoms().isEmpty() ? 0 : 1;\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            context.failure(\"Strategy execution failed: \" + e.getMessage());\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Determines whether modifications should be saved to disk.\n     * Apply goals return true, Check goals return false.\n     */\n    protected abstract boolean shouldSaveModifications();\n\n    /**\n     * Saves the modified documents to disk using domtrip's perfect formatting preservation.\n     */\n    protected void saveModifications(UpgradeContext context, Map<Path, Document> pomMap) {\n        context.info(\"\");\n        context.info(\"Saving modified POMs...\");\n\n        for (Map.Entry<Path, Document> entry : pomMap.entrySet()) {\n            Path pomPath = entry.getKey();\n            Document document = entry.getValue();\n            try {\n                // Use domtrip for perfect formatting preservation\n                String xmlContent = DomUtils.toXml(document);\n                Files.writeString(pomPath, xmlContent);\n                context.detail(\"Saved: \" + pomPath);\n            } catch (Exception e) {\n                context.failure(\"Failed to save \" + pomPath + \": \" + e.getMessage());\n            }\n        }\n\n        context.success(\"All modifications saved successfully\");\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Creates .mvn directory in the root directory if it doesn't exist and the model isn't upgraded to 4.1.0.\n     * This avoids the warning about not being able to find the root directory.\n     */\n    protected void createMvnDirectoryIfNeeded(UpgradeContext context) {\n        context.info(\"\");\n        context.info(\"Creating .mvn directory if needed to avoid root directory warnings...\");\n\n        // Find the root directory (starting directory)\n        Path startingDirectory = context.options().directory().map(Paths::get).orElse(context.invokerRequest.cwd());\n\n        Path mvnDir = startingDirectory.resolve(MVN_DIRECTORY);\n\n        try {\n            if (!Files.exists(mvnDir)) {\n                if (shouldSaveModifications()) {\n                    Files.createDirectories(mvnDir);\n                    context.success(\"Created .mvn directory at \" + mvnDir);\n                } else {\n                    context.action(\"Would create .mvn directory at \" + mvnDir);\n                }\n            } else {\n                context.success(\".mvn directory already exists at \" + mvnDir);\n            }\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            context.failure(\"Failed to create .mvn directory: \" + e.getMessage());\n        }\n    }\n}","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":275,"totalLines":275,"truncated":false}},"title":"impl/maven-cli/src/main/java/org/apache/maven/cling/invoker/mvnup/goals/AbstractUpgradeGoal.java","time":{"start":1782071236342,"end":1782071236442}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_0fa93b045cdc9451016a383fc425d88190bbb2d68c1904e439"}},"id":"prt_eebb916ef001WIWbOPjK2lmx7A","sessionID":"ses_11447590bffeJZbHdUJfcU2BK0","messageID":"msg_eebb90421001FPEMbe0F5WBw4F"}}

eu.maveniverse.domtrip.maven.MavenPomElements.Elements.MODEL_VERSION;\nimport static eu.maveniverse.domtrip.maven.MavenPomElements.ModelVersions.MODEL_VERSION_4_0_0;\nimport static eu.maveniverse.domtrip.maven.MavenPomElements.ModelVersions.MODEL_VERSION_4_1_0;\nimport static eu.maveniverse.domtrip.maven.MavenPomElements.ModelVersions.MODEL_VERSION_4_2_0;\nimport static eu.maveniverse.domtrip.maven.MavenPomElements.Namespaces.MAVEN_4_0_0_NAMESPACE;\nimport static eu.maveniverse.domtrip.maven.MavenPomElements.Namespaces.MAVEN_4_1_0_NAMESPACE;\nimport static eu.maveniverse.domtrip.maven.MavenPomElements.Namespaces.MAVEN_4_2_0_NAMESPACE;\nimport static eu.maveniverse.domtrip.maven.MavenPomElements.SchemaLocations.MAVEN_4_0_0_SCHEMA_LOCATION;\nimport static eu.maveniverse.domtrip.maven.MavenPomElements.SchemaLocations.MAVEN_4_1_0_SCHEMA_LOCATION;\nimport static eu.maveniverse.domtrip.maven.MavenPomElements.SchemaLocations.MAVEN_4_2_0_SCHEMA_LOCATION;\n\n/**\n * Utility class for handling Maven model version operations during upgrades.\n *\n * <p>This class uses domtrip internally for superior formatting preservation\n * and simplified API while maintaining the same external interface.\n */\npublic final class ModelVersionUtils {\n\n    private ModelVersionUtils() {\n        // Utility class\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Detects the model version from a POM document.\n     * Uses both the modelVersion element and namespace URI for detection.\n     *\n     * @param pomDocument the POM document (domtrip Document)\n     * @return the detected model version\n     */\n    public static String detectModelVersion(Document pomDocument) {\n        Editor editor = new Editor(pomDocument);\n        Element root = editor.root();\n        if (root == null) {\n            return MODEL_VERSION_4_0_0;\n        }\n\n        // First try to get from modelVersion element\n        Element modelVersionElement = root.child(MODEL_VERSION).orElse(null);\n        if (modelVersionElement != null) {\n            String modelVersion = modelVersionElement.textContentTrimmed();\n            if (!modelVersion.isEmpty()) {\n                return modelVersion;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Fallback to namespace URI detection\n        String namespaceUri = root.namespaceDeclaration(null);\n        if (MAVEN_4_2_0_NAMESPACE.equals(namespaceUri)) {\n            return MODEL_VERSION_4_2_0;\n        } else if (MAVEN_4_1_0_NAMESPACE.equals(namespaceUri)) {\n            return MODEL_VERSION_4_1_0;\n        } else if (MAVEN_4_0_0_NAMESPACE.equals(namespaceUri)) {\n            return MODEL_VERSION_4_0_0;\n        }\n\n        // Default fallback\n        return MODEL_VERSION_4_0_0;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if a model version is valid for upgrade operations.\n     * Currently supports 4.0.0, 4.1.0, and 4.2.0.\n     *\n     * @param modelVersion the model version to validate\n     * @return true if the model version is valid\n     */\n    public static boolean isValidModelVersion(String modelVersion) {\n        return MODEL_VERSION_4_0_0.equals(modelVersion)\n                || MODEL_VERSION_4_1_0.equals(modelVersion)\n                || MODEL_VERSION_4_2_0.equals(modelVersion);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if an upgrade from one version to another is possible.\n     *\n     * @param fromVersion the source version\n     * @param toVersion the target version\n     * @return true if the upgrade is possible\n     */\n    public static boolean canUpgrade(String fromVersion, String toVersion) {\n        if (fromVersion == null || toVersion == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Support upgrades: 4.0.0 → 4.1.0, 4.0.0 → 4.2.0, 4.1.0 → 4.2.0\n        if (MODEL_VERSION_4_0_0.equals(fromVersion)) {\n            return MODEL_VERSION_4_1_0.equals(toVersion) || MODEL_VERSION_4_2_0.equals(toVersion);\n        }\n        if (MODEL_VERSION_4_1_0.equals(fromVersion)) {\n            return MODEL_VERSION_4_2_0.equals(toVersion);\n        }\n\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if a model version is eligible for inference optimizations.\n     * Models 4.0.0+ are eligible (4.0.0 has limited inference, 4.1.0+ has full inference).\n     *\n     * @param modelVersion the model version to check\n     * @return true if eligible for inference\n     */\n    public static boolean isEligibleForInference(String modelVersion) {\n        return MODEL_VERSION_4_0_0.equals(modelVersion)\n                || MODEL_VERSION_4_1_0.equals(modelVersion)\n                || MODEL_VERSION_4_2_0.equals(modelVersion);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if a model version is newer than 4.1.0.\n     *\n     * @param modelVersion the model version to check\n     * @return true if newer than 4.1.0\n     */\n    public static boolean isNewerThan410(String modelVersion) {\n        if (modelVersion == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Simple version comparison for now\n        // This could be enhanced with proper version parsing if needed\n        try {\n            String[] parts = modelVersion.split(\"\\\\.\");\n            if (parts.length >= 2) {\n                int major = Integer.parseInt(parts[0]);\n                int minor = Integer.parseInt(parts[1]);\n\n                if (major > 4) {\n                    return true;\n                }\n                if (major == 4 && minor > 1) {\n                    return true;\n                }\n                if (major == 4 && minor == 1 && parts.length > 2) {\n                    int patch = Integer.parseInt(parts[2]);\n                    return patch > 0;\n                }\n            }\n        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {\n            // If we can't parse it, assume it's not newer\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if a model version is greater than or equal to a target version.\n     *\n     * @param modelVersion the model version to check\n     * @param targetVersion the target version to compare against\n     * @return true if modelVersion >= targetVersion\n     */\n    public static boolean isVersionGreaterOrEqual(String modelVersion, String targetVersion) {\n        if (modelVersion == null || targetVersion == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Handle exact equality first\n        if (modelVersion.equals(targetVersion)) {\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        // For now, handle the specific cases we need\n        if (MODEL_VERSION_4_1_0.equals(targetVersion)) {\n            return isNewerThan410(modelVersion);\n        }\n\n        // Default to false for unknown comparisons\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Updates the model version element in a POM document.\n     *\n     * @param pomDocument the POM document (domtrip Document)\n     * @param newVersion the new model version\n     */\n    public static void updateModelVersion(Document pomDocument, String newVersion) {\n        Editor editor = new Editor(pomDocument);\n        Element root = editor.root();\n        if (root == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        Element modelVersionElement = root.child(MODEL_VERSION).orElse(null);\n        if (modelVersionElement != null) {\n            editor.setTextContent(modelVersionElement, newVersion);\n        } else {\n            // Create new modelVersion element if it doesn't exist\n            // domtrip will automatically handle proper positioning and formatting\n            DomUtils.insertContentElement(root, MODEL_VERSION, newVersion);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Removes the model version element from a POM editor.\n     * This is used during inference when the model version can be inferred.\n     *\n     * @param document the XML document\n     * @return true if the element was removed, false if it didn't exist\n     */\n    public static boolean removeModelVersion(Document document) {\n        Element root = document.root();\n        if (root == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        Element modelVersionElement = root.child(MODEL_VERSION).orElse(null);\n        if (modelVersionElement != null) {\n            return root.removeNode(modelVersionElement);\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets the schema location for a model version.\n     *\n     * @param modelVersion the model version\n     * @return the schema location\n     */\n    public static String getSchemaLocationForModelVersion(String modelVersion) {\n        if (MODEL_VERSION_4_2_0.equals(modelVersion)) {\n            return MAVEN_4_2_0_SCHEMA_LOCATION;\n        } else if (MODEL_VERSION_4_1_0.equals(modelVersion)) {\n            return MAVEN_4_1_0_SCHEMA_LOCATION;\n        } else if (isNewerThan410(modelVersion)) {\n            // For versions newer than 4.1.0 but not dependencyManagement section\n        Element dependencyManagement = root.child(DEPENDENCY_MANAGEMENT).orElse(null);\n        if (dependencyManagement != null) {\n            Element dependencies = dependencyManagement.child(DEPENDENCIES).orElse(null);\n            if (dependencies != null) {\n                hasChanges |=\n                        removeManagedDependenciesFromSection(context,               .reduce(false, Boolean::logicalOr);\n\n        hasChanges |= profileChanges;\n\n        return hasChanges;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Applies dependency inference redundancy   * Applies limited parent inference for 4.0.0 models.\n     * Only removes child groupId/version when they match parent.\n     */\n    private boolean trimParentElementLimited(UpgradeContext context, Element root, Element parentElement) {\n        boolean hasChanges = false;\n\n        // Get parent GAV\n        String parentGroupId = parentElement.childText(MavenPomElements.Elements.GROUP_ID);\n        String parentVersion = parentElement.childText(MavenPomElements.Elements.VERSION);\n\n        // Get child GAV\n        String childGroupId =  * Removes parent groupId/version/artifactId when they can be inferred.\n     */\n    private boolean trimParentElementFull(\n            UpgradeContext context, Element root, Element parentElement, Map<Path, Document> pomMap) {\n        boolean hasChanges = false;\n\n        // Get child GAV before applying any changes\n        String childGroupId = context.detail(\"Removed: parent version (child has no explicit version)\");\n                    hasChanges = true;\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Remove parent artifactId if it parentElement.child(ARTIFACT_ID).orElse(null);\n                if (parentArtifactIdElement != null) {\n                    DomUtils.removeElement(parentArtifactIdElement);\n                    context.detail(\"Removed: parent artifactId (can be inferred from relativePath)\");\n                    hasChanges = true;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        return hasChanges;\n private boolean isParentInReactor(Element parentElement, Map<Path, Document> pomMap, UpgradeContext context) {\n        // If relativePath is explicitly set to empty, parent is definitely external\n        String relativePath = parentElement.childText(MavenPomElements.Elements.RELATIVE_PATH);\n        if (relativePath != null && relativePath.trim().isEmpty()) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Extract parent GAV\n        String parentGroupId = parentElement.childText(MavenPomElements.Elements.GROUP_ID);\n        String parentArtifactId = parentElement.childText(MavenPomElements.Elements.ARTIFACT_ID);\n        String parentVersion = parentElement.childText(MavenPomElements.Elements.VERSION);\n\n        if (parentGroupId == null || parentArtifactId == null || parentVersion == null) {\n            // Cannot determine parent GAV, assume external\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        Coordinates parentGAV = Coordinates.of(parentGroupId, parentArtifactId, parentVersion);\n\n        // Check if any POM in our reactor matches the parent GAV using GAVUtils\n        for (Document pomDocument : pomMap.values()) {\n            Coordinates pomGAV =\n                    AbstractUpgradeStrategy.extractArtifactCoordinatesWithParentResolution(context, pomDocument);\n            if (pomGAV != null && pomGAV.equals(parentGAV)) {\n                return true;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Parent not found in reactor, must be external\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Determines if parent artifactId can be inferred from relativePath.\n     */\n    private boolean canInferParentArtifactId(Element parentElement, Map<Path, Document> pomMap) {\n        // Get relativePath (default is \"../pom.xml\" if not specified)\n        String relativePath = parentElement.childText(MavenPomElements.Elements.RELATIVE_PATH);\n        if (relativePath == null || relativePath.trim().isEmpty()) {\n            relativePath = DEFAULT_PARENT_RELATIVE_PATH; // Maven default\n        }\n\n        // Only infer artifactId if relativePath is the default and we have multiple POMs\n        // indicating this is likely a multi-module project\n        return DEFAULT_PARENT_RELATIVE_PATH.equals(relativePath) && pomMap.size() > 1;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if a redundant\n        }\n\n        // Get the directory containing this POM\n        Path parentDir = pomPath.getParent();\n        if (parentDir == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n actualSubprojects.add(dir.getFileName().toString()));\n                }\n            }\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            // If we can't read the directory, assume not redundant\n
